    <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p><code>yield</code> and <code>await</code> are permitted as <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in the grammar, and prohibited with <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-static-semantic-rules"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-static-semantic-rules">static semantics</a></emu-xref> below, to prohibit automatic semicolon insertion in cases such as</p>
      <pre><code class="hljs javascript">
        <span class="hljs-keyword">let</span>
        <span class="hljs-keyword">await</span> <span class="hljs-number">0</span>;
      </code></pre>
    </div></emu-note>

<emu-production id="prod-BindingIdentifier" name="BindingIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>await</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-LabelIdentifier" name="LabelIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-LabelIdentifier">LabelIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>await</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <ul>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars">goal symbol</a></emu-xref> of the syntactic grammar is <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>.
        </li>
      </ul>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-BindingIdentifier" name="BindingIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>yield</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <ul>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if this production has a <sub>[Yield]</sub> parameter.
        </li>
      </ul>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-BindingIdentifier" name="BindingIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>await</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <ul>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if this production has an <sub>[Await]</sub> parameter.
        </li>
      </ul>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-IdentifierReference" name="IdentifierReference" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-IdentifierReference">IdentifierReference</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-BindingIdentifier" name="BindingIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-LabelIdentifier" name="LabelIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-LabelIdentifier">LabelIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <ul>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if this production has a <sub>[Yield]</sub> parameter and the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>"yield"</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if this production has an <sub>[Await]</sub> parameter and the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>"await"</emu-val>.
        </li>
      </ul>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-Identifier" name="Identifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> but not <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-ReservedWord">ReservedWord</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <ul>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(this phrase) is <emu-val>true</emu-val> and the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> is one of <emu-val>"implements"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"interface"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"let"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"package"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"private"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"protected"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"public"</emu-val>, <emu-val>"static"</emu-val>, or <emu-val>"yield"</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars">goal symbol</a></emu-xref> of the syntactic grammar is <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt> and the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>"await"</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> is the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of any <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-ReservedWord">ReservedWord</a></emu-nt> except for <code>yield</code> or <code>await</code>.
        </li>
      </ul>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> normalizes any Unicode escape sequences in <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> hence such escapes cannot be used to write an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt> whose code point sequence is the same as a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-ReservedWord">ReservedWord</a></emu-nt>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>In <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#non-strict-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#non-strict-code">non-strict code</a></emu-xref>, the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-keywords-and-reserved-words"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-keywords-and-reserved-words">keyword</a></emu-xref> <code>yield</code> may be used as an identifier. Evaluating the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-IdentifierReference">IdentifierReference</a></emu-nt> resolves the binding of <code>yield</code> as if it was an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt>. Early Error restriction ensures that such an evaluation only can occur for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#non-strict-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#non-strict-code">non-strict code</a></emu-xref>.</p>

    <h2>Supplemental Syntax</h2>
    <p>
      When processing an instance of the production<br>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-PrimaryExpression" name="PrimaryExpression" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PrimaryExpression">PrimaryExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar><br>
      the interpretation of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a></emu-nt> is refined using the following grammar:
    </p>

<emu-production id="prod-MultiplicativeOperator" name="MultiplicativeOperator"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MultiplicativeOperator">MultiplicativeOperator</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-oneof>one of</emu-oneof>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-t>/</emu-t> <emu-t>%</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
    <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
      <ul>
        <li>The <code>*</code> operator performs multiplication, producing the product of its operands.</li>
        <li>The <code>/</code> operator performs division, producing the quotient of its operands.</li>
        <li>The <code>%</code> operator yields the remainder of its operands from an implied division.</li>
      </ul>
    </div></emu-note>

e5:T85c,<h2>Syntax</h2>
    <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ConditionalExpression" name="ConditionalExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ConditionalExpression">ConditionalExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[In, Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ShortCircuitExpression">ShortCircuitExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?In, ?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ShortCircuitExpression">ShortCircuitExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?In, ?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>?</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-AssignmentExpression">AssignmentExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[+In, ?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>:</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-AssignmentExpression">AssignmentExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?In, ?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
    <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>The grammar for a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ConditionalExpression">ConditionalExpression</a></emu-nt> in ECMAScript is slightly different from that in C and Java, which each allow the second subexpression to be an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Expression">Expression</a></emu-nt> but restrict the third expression to be a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ConditionalExpression">ConditionalExpression</a></emu-nt>. The motivation for this difference in ECMAScript is to allow an assignment expression to be governed by either arm of a conditional and to eliminate the confusing and fairly useless case of a comma expression as the centre expression.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

ef:T1466,<emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>An <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ArrayLiteral">ArrayLiteral</a></emu-nt> is an expression describing the initialization of an Array, using a list, of zero or more expressions each of which represents an array element, enclosed in square brackets. The elements need not be literals; they are evaluated each time the array initializer is evaluated.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <p>Array elements may be elided at the beginning, middle or end of the element list. Whenever a comma in the element list is not preceded by an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-AssignmentExpression">AssignmentExpression</a></emu-nt> (i.e., a comma at the beginning or after another comma), the missing array element contributes to the length of the Array and increases the index of subsequent elements. Elided array elements are not defined. If an element is elided at the end of an array, that element does not contribute to the length of the Array.</p>
      <h2>Syntax</h2>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ArrayLiteral" name="ArrayLiteral"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ArrayLiteral">ArrayLiteral</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>[</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>]</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>[</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ElementList">ElementList</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>]</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>[</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ElementList">ElementList</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>,</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>]</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

f2:T2074,<emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>An object initializer is an expression describing the initialization of an Object, written in a form resembling a literal. It is a list of zero or more pairs of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property keys</a></emu-xref> and associated values, enclosed in curly brackets. The values need not be literals; they are evaluated each time the object initializer is evaluated.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <h2>Syntax</h2>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ObjectLiteral" name="ObjectLiteral"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ObjectLiteral">ObjectLiteral</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinitionList">PropertyDefinitionList</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinitionList">PropertyDefinitionList</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>,</emu-t> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-PropertyDefinitionList" name="PropertyDefinitionList"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinitionList">PropertyDefinitionList</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinition">PropertyDefinition</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinitionList">PropertyDefinitionList</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>,</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinition">PropertyDefinition</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-PropertyName" name="PropertyName"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyName">PropertyName</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield, Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-LiteralPropertyName">LiteralPropertyName</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ComputedPropertyName">ComputedPropertyName</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, ?Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production>

      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-MethodDefinition">MethodDefinition</a></emu-nt> is defined in <emu-xref href="#sec-method-definitions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-method-definitions">15.4</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>In certain contexts, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ObjectLiteral">ObjectLiteral</a></emu-nt> is used as a cover grammar for a more restricted secondary grammar. The <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverInitializedName">CoverInitializedName</a></emu-nt> production is necessary to fully cover these secondary grammars. However, use of this production results in an early Syntax Error in normal contexts where an actual <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ObjectLiteral">ObjectLiteral</a></emu-nt> is expected.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

fa:Tacb,<p>The abstract operation IsValidRegularExpressionLiteral takes argument <var>literal</var> (a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-RegularExpressionLiteral">RegularExpressionLiteral</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Boolean. It determines if its argument is a valid regular expression literal. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>flags</var> be the <emu-xref aoid="FlagText"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-static-semantics-flagtext">FlagText</a></emu-xref> of <var>literal</var>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>flags</var> contains any code points other than <code>d</code>, <code>g</code>, <code>i</code>, <code>m</code>, <code>s</code>, <code>u</code>, <code>v</code>, or <code>y</code>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>flags</var> contains any code point more than once, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li>If <var>flags</var> contains <code>u</code>, let <var>u</var> be <emu-val>true</emu-val>; else let <var>u</var> be <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li>If <var>flags</var> contains <code>v</code>, let <var>v</var> be <emu-val>true</emu-val>; else let <var>v</var> be <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li>Let <var>patternText</var> be the <emu-xref aoid="BodyText"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-static-semantics-bodytext">BodyText</a></emu-xref> of <var>literal</var>.</li><li>If <var>u</var> is <emu-val>false</emu-val> and <var>v</var> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>, then<ol><li>Let <var>stringValue</var> be <emu-xref aoid="CodePointsToString"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-codepointstostring">CodePointsToString</a></emu-xref>(<var>patternText</var>).</li><li>Set <var>patternText</var> to the sequence of code points resulting from interpreting each of the 16-bit elements of <var>stringValue</var> as a Unicode BMP code point. UTF-16 decoding is not applied to the elements.</li></ol></li><li>Let <var>parseResult</var> be <emu-xref aoid="ParsePattern"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/text-processing#sec-parsepattern">ParsePattern</a></emu-xref>(<var>patternText</var>, <var>u</var>, <var>v</var>).</li><li class="exit">If <var>parseResult</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

105:Ta1d,<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-OptionalChain" name="OptionalChain"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-OptionalChain">OptionalChain</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>?.</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-TemplateLiteral">TemplateLiteral</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-OptionalChain">OptionalChain</a></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-TemplateLiteral">TemplateLiteral</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
        <ul>
          <li>
            It is a Syntax Error if any source text is matched by this production.
          </li>
        </ul>
        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>This production exists in order to prevent automatic semicolon insertion rules (<emu-xref href="#sec-automatic-semicolon-insertion"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-automatic-semicolon-insertion">12.10</a></emu-xref>) from being applied to the following code:</p>
          <pre><code class="hljs javascript">
            a?.<span class="hljs-property">b</span>
            <span class="hljs-string">`c`</span>
          </code></pre>
          <p>so that it would be interpreted as two valid statements. The purpose is to maintain consistency with similar code without optional chaining:</p>
          <pre><code class="hljs javascript">
            a.<span class="hljs-property">b</span>
            <span class="hljs-string">`c`</span>
          </code></pre>
          <p>which is a valid statement and where automatic semicolon insertion does not apply.</p>
        </div></emu-note>

106:Tbcb,<emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Properties are accessed by name, using either the dot notation:</p>
        <div class="rhs">
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>.</code> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt><br>
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CallExpression">CallExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>.</code> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt>
        </div>
        <p>or the bracket notation:</p>
        <div class="rhs">
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>[</code> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Expression">Expression</a></emu-nt> <code>]</code><br>
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CallExpression">CallExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>[</code> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Expression">Expression</a></emu-nt> <code>]</code>
        </div>
        <p>The dot notation is explained by the following syntactic conversion:</p>
        <div class="rhs">
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>.</code> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt>
        </div>
        <p>is identical in its behaviour to</p>
        <div class="rhs">
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>[</code> &lt;<i>identifier-name-string</i>> <code>]</code>
        </div>
        <p>and similarly</p>
        <div class="rhs">
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CallExpression">CallExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>.</code> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt>
        </div>
        <p>is identical in its behaviour to</p>
        <div class="rhs">
          <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CallExpression">CallExpression</a></emu-nt> <code>[</code> &lt;<i>identifier-name-string</i>> <code>]</code>
        </div>
        <p>where &lt;<i>identifier-name-string</i>> is the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

        <ul>
          <li>
            It is a Syntax Error if <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(the <emu-nt><a             <p>
              It is a Syntax Error if the derived <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-UnaryExpression">UnaryExpression</a></emu-nt> is<br>
              <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-PrimaryExpression" name="PrimaryExpression" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PrimaryExpression">PrimaryExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar><br>

12b:Tecd,<p>The abstract operation InstanceofOperator takes arguments <var>value</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref>) and <var>target</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref>) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> a Boolean or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>. It implements the generic algorithm for determining if <var>value</var> is an instance of <var>target</var> either by consulting <var>target</var>'s <emu-intrinsic>%Symbol.hasInstance%</emu-intrinsic> method or, if absent, determining whether the value of <var>target</var>'s <emu-val>"prototype"</emu-val> property is present in <var>value</var>'s prototype chain. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>target</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">is not an Object</a></emu-xref>, throw a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception.</li><li>Let <var>instOfHandler</var> be ? <emu-xref aoid="GetMethod"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-getmethod">GetMethod</a></emu-xref>(<var>target</var>, <emu-intrinsic>%Symbol.hasInstance%</emu-intrinsic>).</li><li>If <var>instOfHandler</var> is not <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>, then<ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-xref aoid="ToBoolean"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-toboolean">ToBoolean</a></emu-xref>(? <emu-xref aoid="Call"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-call">Call</a></emu-xref>(<var>instOfHandler</var>, <var>target</var>, « <var>value</var> »)).</li></ol></li><li id="step-instanceof-check-function" class="exit">If <emu-xref aoid="IsCallable"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-iscallable">IsCallable</a></emu-xref>(<var>target</var>) is <emu-val>false</emu-val>, throw a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception.</li><li id="step-instanceof-fallback" class="exit">Return ? <emu-xref aoid="OrdinaryHasInstance"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-ordinaryhasinstance">OrdinaryHasInstance</a></emu-xref>(<var>target</var>, <var>value</var>).</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Steps <emu-xref href="#step-instanceof-check-function"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#step-instanceof-check-function">4</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref href="#step-instanceof-fallback"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#step-instanceof-fallback">5</a></emu-xref> provide compatibility with previous editions of ECMAScript that did not use a <emu-intrinsic>%Symbol.hasInstance%</emu-intrinsic> method to define the <code>instanceof</code> operator semantics. If an object does not define or inherit <emu-intrinsic>%Symbol.hasInstance%</emu-intrinsic> it uses the default <code>instanceof</code> semantics.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The equality operators maintain the following invariants:</p>
        <ul>
          <li>
            <code>A != B</code> is equivalent to <code>!(A == B)</code>.
          </li>
          <li>
            <code>A == B</code> is equivalent to <code>B == A</code>, except in the order of evaluation of <code>A</code> and <code>B</code>.
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The equality operator is not always transitive. For example, there might be two distinct String objects, each representing the same String value; each String object would be considered equal to the String value by the <code>==</code> operator, but the two String objects would not be equal to each other. For example:</p>
        <ul>
          <li>
            <code>new String("a") == "a"</code> and <code>"a" == new String("a")</code> are both <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
          </li>
          <li>
            <code>new String("a") == new String("a")</code> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>.
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Comparison of Strings uses a simple equality test on sequences of code unit values. There is no attempt to use the more complex, semantically oriented definitions of character or string equality and collating order defined in the Unicode specification. Therefore Strings values that are canonically equal according to the Unicode Standard could test as unequal. In effect this algorithm assumes that both Strings are already in normalized form.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

        <li>

        </li>
      </ul>

      <ul>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref aoid="AssignmentTargetType"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-assignmenttargettype">AssignmentTargetType</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-LeftHandSideExpression">LeftHandSideExpression</a></emu-nt> is not <emu-const>simple</emu-const>.
        </li>

        <p>When this expression occurs within <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code">strict mode code</a></emu-xref>, it is a runtime error if <var>leftRef</var> in step <emu-xref href="#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-simple-putvalue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-simple-putvalue">1.e</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref href="#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-compound-getvalue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-compound-getvalue">3</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref href="#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-lgcl-and-getvalue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-lgcl-and-getvalue">2</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref href="#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-lgcl-or-getvalue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-lgcl-or-getvalue">2</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref href="#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-lgcl-nullish-getvalue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-lgcl-nullish-getvalue">2</a></emu-xref> is an unresolvable reference. In these cases a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception is thrown.</p>

133:T2a11,<h2>Supplemental Syntax</h2>
      <p>
        In certain circumstances when processing an instance of the production<br>

        the interpretation of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-LeftHandSideExpression">LeftHandSideExpression</a></emu-nt> is refined using the following grammar:

13a:T31b1,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> ArrayAccumulation takes arguments <var>array</var> (an Array) and <var>nextIndex</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref>) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref> or an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">abrupt completion</a></emu-xref>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
        <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-Elision" name="Elision" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>,</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>length</var> be <var>nextIndex</var> + 1.</li><li>Perform ? <emu-xref aoid="Set"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-set-o-p-v-throw">Set</a></emu-xref>(<var>array</var>, <emu-val>"length"</emu-val>, <emu-xref aoid="𝔽"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#𝔽">𝔽</a></emu-xref>(<var>length</var>), <emu-val>true</emu-val>).</li><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#note-step"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#note-step">NOTE</a></emu-xref>: The above step throws if <var>length</var> exceeds 2<sup>32</sup> - 1.</li><li class="exit">Return <var>length</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
        <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-Elision" name="Elision" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>,</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
        <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return ? ArrayAccumulation of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a></emu-nt> with arguments <var>array</var> and (<var>nextIndex</var> + 1).</li></ol></emu-alg>
        <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ElementList" name="ElementList" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ElementList">ElementList</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-AssignmentExpression">AssignmentExpression</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Elision">Elision</a></emu-nt> is present, then<ol><li>Set <var>nextIndex</var> to ? It performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>If <var>raw</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, then<ol><li>Let <var>string</var> be the <emu-xref aoid="TRV"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-static-semantics-trv">TRV</a></emu-xref> of <var>templateToken</var>.</li></ol></li><li>Else,<ol><li>Let <var>string</var> be the <emu-xref aoid="TV"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-static-semantics-tv">TV</a></emu-xref> of <var>templateToken</var>.</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <var>string</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
        <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>This operation returns <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> if <var>raw</var> is <emu-val>false</emu-val> and <var>templateToken</var> contains a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-NotEscapeSequence">NotEscapeSequence</a></emu-nt>. In all other cases, it returns a String.</p>

        <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>The creation of a template object cannot result in an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">abrupt completion</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        </div></emu-note>
        <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>Each <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-TemplateLiteral">TemplateLiteral</a></emu-nt> in the program code of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> is associated with a unique template object that is used in the evaluation of tagged Templates (<emu-xref href="#sec-template-literals-runtime-semantics-evaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-template-literals-runtime-semantics-evaluation">13.2.8.6</a></emu-xref>). The template objects are frozen and the same template object is used each time a specific tagged Template is evaluated. Whether template objects are created lazily upon first evaluation of the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-TemplateLiteral">TemplateLiteral</a></emu-nt> or eagerly prior to first evaluation is an implementation choice that is not observable to ECMAScript code.</p>
        </div></emu-note>
        <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
          <p>Future editions of this specification may define additional non-enumerable properties of template objects.</p>
        </div></emu-note>

150:T62e,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> abstract operation HostFinalizeImportMeta takes arguments <var>importMeta</var> (an Object) and <var>moduleRecord</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>) and returns <emu-const>unused</emu-const>. It allows <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> to perform any extraordinary operations to prepare the object returned from <code>import.meta</code>.</p>

          <p>Most <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> will be able to simply define <emu-xref aoid="HostGetImportMetaProperties"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-hostgetimportmetaproperties">HostGetImportMetaProperties</a></emu-xref>, and leave HostFinalizeImportMeta with its default behaviour. However, HostFinalizeImportMeta provides an “escape hatch” for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> which need to directly manipulate the object before it is exposed to ECMAScript code.</p>

          <p>The default implementation of HostFinalizeImportMeta is to return <emu-const>unused</emu-const>.</p>

<emu-xref aoid="NamedEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-runtime-semantics-namedevaluation">NamedEvaluation</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Initializer">Initializer</a></emu-nt> with argument <var>target</var>.</li></ol></li><li>Else,<ol><li>Let <var>defaultValue</var> be ? <emu-xref aoid="Evaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-evaluation">Evaluation</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Initializer">Initializer</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Let <var>rhsValue</var> be ? <emu-xref aoid="GetValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-getvalue">GetValue</a></emu-xref>(<var>defaultValue</var>).</li></ol></li></ol></li><li>Else,<ol><li>Let <var>rhsValue</var> be <var>value</var>.</li></ol></li><li>If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-DestructuringAssignmentTarget">DestructuringAssignmentTarget</a></emu-nt> is either an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ObjectLiteral">ObjectLiteral</a></emu-nt> or an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ArrayLiteral">ArrayLiteral</a></emu-nt>, then<ol><li>Let <var>assignmentPattern</var> be the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-AssignmentPattern">AssignmentPattern</a></emu-nt> that is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">covered</a></emu-xref> by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-DestructuringAssignmentTarget">DestructuringAssignmentTarget</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return ? <emu-xref aoid="DestructuringAssignmentEvaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-runtime-semantics-destructuringassignmentevaluation">DestructuringAssignmentEvaluation</a></emu-xref> of <var>assignmentPattern</var> with argument <var>rhsValue</var>.</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return ? <emu-xref aoid="PutValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-putvalue">PutValue</a></emu-xref>(<var>leftRef</var>, <var>rhsValue</var>).</li></ol></emu-alg>
